⏺ Perfect! That makes a lot of sense - keeping the spatial/layout data separate from the textual/content data while still in one file. This gives you clean separation of concerns within a unified structure.

  Proposed Structure

  {
    "version": "2.0",
    "metadata": {
      "sourcePdf": "document.pdf",
      "createdAt": "2025-07-16T10:00:00Z",
      "lastEditedAt": "2025-07-16T11:30:00Z"
    },

    // Spatial/layout data (from Mark stage)
    "regions": [
      {
        "id": "region1",
        "pageNumber": 1,
        "x": 0.1, "y": 0.2, "width": 0.8, "height": 0.1,
        "semanticType": "title",
        "label": "Chapter heading"
      },
      {
        "id": "region2",
        "pageNumber": 1,
        "x": 0.1, "y": 0.4, "width": 0.8, "height": 0.3,
        "semanticType": "paragraph",
        "label": "First paragraph"
      }
    ],

    // Textual/content data (from Edit stage)
    "content": {
      "regions": {
        "region1": {
          "ocrText": "Chapter One",
          "editedText": "<h1>Chapter One</h1>",
          "ocrConfidence": 0.95,
          "lastEditedAt": "2025-07-16T11:15:00Z"
        },
        "region2": {
          "ocrText": "In the beginning was the word...",
          "editedText": "<p>In the beginning was the word...</p>",
          "ocrConfidence": 0.88,
          "lastEditedAt": "2025-07-16T11:20:00Z"
        }
      },

      // Document structure/ordering
      "structure": {
        "type": "document",
        "title": "region1",
        "sections": [
          {
            "type": "chapter",
            "heading": "region1",
            "content": ["region2", "region3", "region4"]
          }
        ]
      }
    }
  }

  Benefits of This Structure

  ✅ Clean Separation

  - regions[]: Pure spatial/layout data (Mark stage)
  - content.regions{}: Pure textual data (Edit stage)
  - content.structure{}: Document organization (Edit stage)

  ✅ Easy Stage-by-Stage Development

  // Stage 1: Only regions exist
  const doc = { regions: [...] };

  // Stage 2: Add content fields
  const doc = { regions: [...], content: { regions: {...}, structure: {...} } };

  ✅ Flexible References

  - Content can reference regions by ID
  - Structure can reorder/reorganize without touching spatial data
  - Easy to find "orphaned" regions (in regions[] but not in content.regions{})

  ✅ Independent Evolution

  - Can update OCR without touching layout
  - Can restructure document without re-OCRing
  - Can add new regions without affecting existing content

  Data Flow Examples

  Mark Stage → Edit Stage

  // User marks regions
  regions = [{id: "region1", x: 0.1, ...}];

  // OCR processes each region
  content.regions["region1"] = {
    ocrText: await ocrRegion("region1"),
    editedText: ocrText, // initially same
    ocrConfidence: 0.95
  };

  View Stage Toggle

  function getRegionDisplay(regionId, mode) {
    const region = regions.find(r => r.id === regionId);
    const content = content.regions[regionId];

    return mode === 'original'
      ? extractPdfCrop(region)  // Show PDF crop
      : content?.editedText;    // Show enhanced text
  }

  This structure feels really clean and extensible. Does this capture what you had in mind?


> More or less, yes. Do you know how Wikisource does it? How does it represent the text of a work, with reference to the pages it contains etc?


⏺ Great question! Wikisource has a really sophisticated approach that's quite relevant to what you're building.

  Wikisource's Approach

  Wikisource uses a two-layer system:

  1. Page Namespace (Layout/Source)

  Each page of the source document gets its own wiki page:
  - Page:Book_Title.pdf/1 - First page
  - Page:Book_Title.pdf/2 - Second page
  - etc.

  Each page contains:
  <noinclude>
  {{PageQuality|1|user}}
  {{RunningHeader|left=Chapter 1|center=|right=23}}
  </noinclude>

  <section begin=chapter1 />
  = Chapter One =

  In the beginning was the word, and the word was with God...
  <section end=chapter1 />

  <section begin=paragraph1 />
  This is the first paragraph of the chapter content...
  <section end=paragraph1 />

  2. Main Namespace (Logical/Reading)

  The actual readable work is assembled by transcluding sections from pages:
  {{header|title=Book Title|author=Author Name}}

  == Chapter 1 ==
  {{Page:Book_Title.pdf/23|chapter1}}

  {{Page:Book_Title.pdf/23|paragraph1}}
  {{Page:Book_Title.pdf/24|paragraph1_continued}}

  Key Insights from Wikisource

  ✅ Separation of Concerns

  - Pages: Focus on faithful transcription of source layout
  - Sections: Named regions that can span page boundaries
  - Works: Logical reading order, can reorder/reorganize sections

  ✅ Named Sections with Boundaries

  - <section begin=name /> and <section end=name />
  - Allows precise references to text spans
  - Text can span multiple pages
  - Same text can be included in multiple contexts

  ✅ Flexible Assembly

  - Final document built by referencing sections
  - Can create different "views" (complete work, chapter only, etc.)
  - Can handle complex layouts (footnotes, multi-column, etc.)

  Adapting This to Your Approach

  This maps really well to your structure! You could extend it like:

  {
    "regions": [
      {
        "id": "region1",
        "pageNumber": 23,
        "x": 0.1, "y": 0.2, "width": 0.8, "height": 0.1,
        "semanticType": "title",
        "label": "Chapter heading",
        "sectionName": "chapter1_title"  // Named reference
      }
    ],

    "content": {
      "sections": {
        "chapter1_title": {
          "regions": ["region1"],
          "ocrText": "Chapter One",
          "editedText": "<h1>Chapter One</h1>"
        },
        "chapter1_intro": {
          "regions": ["region2", "region3"],  // Spans multiple regions!
          "ocrText": "In the beginning...",
          "editedText": "<p>In the beginning was the word...</p>"
        }
      },

      "structure": {
        "title": "Book Title",
        "chapters": [
          {
            "title": "chapter1_title",
            "content": ["chapter1_intro", "chapter1_body"]
          }
        ]
      }
    }
  }

  Benefits of Section-Based Approach

  - Logical units: Sections represent meaningful text units
  - Flexible spanning: One section can include multiple regions
  - Reusable content: Same section can appear in multiple contexts
  - Clean references: Structure references sections, not individual regions

  This feels like it could give you the same flexibility as Wikisource while maintaining your clean separation between spatial and textual data. What do you think of this section-based approach?

⏺ Yes! That's a brilliant approach - using ProseMirror with custom nodes/marks that reference regions. This gives you the best of both worlds:

  ProseMirror Document with Region References

  <!-- Rendered ProseMirror document -->
  <h1><region id="region1">Chapter 1</region></h1>

  <p>
    <region id="region2">In the beginning</region>
    <region id="region3">was the word</region>
    <region id="region4">, and the word was good.</region>
  </p>

  <blockquote>
    <region id="region5">This is a quoted passage that spans multiple lines and represents a single semantic unit from the PDF.</region>
  </blockquote>

  ProseMirror Schema Implementation

  // Custom node/mark for region references
  const regionMark = {
    attrs: { regionId: { default: null } },
    parseDOM: [{
      tag: "region",
      getAttrs: (node) => ({ regionId: node.getAttribute("id") })
    }],
    toDOM: (mark) => ["region", { id: mark.attrs.regionId }, 0]
  };

  const schema = new Schema({
    nodes: addListNodes(basicSchema.spec.nodes, "paragraph block*", "block"),
    marks: basicSchema.spec.marks.append({
      region: regionMark
    })
  });

  Data Flow

  Mark Stage: Create regions

  {
    "regions": [
      { "id": "region1", "pageNumber": 1, "x": 0.1, "y": 0.2, ... },
      { "id": "region2", "pageNumber": 1, "x": 0.1, "y": 0.4, ... }
    ]
  }

  Edit Stage: Build ProseMirror document

  {
    "regions": [...],
    "content": {
      "prosemirrorDoc": {
        "type": "doc",
        "content": [
          {
            "type": "heading",
            "attrs": { "level": 1 },
            "content": [
              {
                "type": "text",
                "text": "Chapter 1",
                "marks": [{ "type": "region", "attrs": { "regionId": "region1" } }]
              }
            ]
          },
          {
            "type": "paragraph",
            "content": [
              {
                "type": "text",
                "text": "In the beginning",
                "marks": [{ "type": "region", "attrs": { "regionId": "region2" } }]
              },
              { "type": "text", "text": " " },
              {
                "type": "text",
                "text": "was the word",
                "marks": [{ "type": "region", "attrs": { "regionId": "region3" } }]
              }
            ]
          }
        ]
      }
    }
  }

  Powerful Features This Enables

  ✅ Rich Text Editing

  - Full ProseMirror capabilities (bold, italic, links, etc.)
  - Structural editing (headings, lists, quotes)
  - Collaborative editing potential

  ✅ Bidirectional Region Mapping

  // Find all regions in a paragraph
  function getRegionsInNode(node) {
    const regions = [];
    node.descendants((child) => {
      child.marks.forEach(mark => {
        if (mark.type.name === 'region') {
          regions.push(mark.attrs.regionId);
        }
      });
    });
    return regions;
  }

  // Highlight corresponding PDF regions when user selects text
  editor.on('selectionUpdate', ({ editor }) => {
    const selectedRegions = getRegionsInSelection(editor.state.selection);
    highlightPDFRegions(selectedRegions);
  });

  ✅ Flexible Content Organization

  - Regions can be reordered by editing the document
  - Multiple regions can be combined into single paragraphs
  - Single regions can be split across multiple elements
  - Text can be added that doesn't correspond to any region

  ✅ Smart OCR Integration

  // Initially populate from OCR
  async function createInitialDocument(regions) {
    const doc = schema.node('doc', null, []);

    for (const region of regions) {
      const ocrText = await performOCR(region);
      const textNode = schema.text(ocrText, [
        schema.marks.region.create({ regionId: region.id })
      ]);
      // Add to document...
    }

    return doc;
  }

  ✅ View Tab Magic

  In the View tab, each <region> element can toggle between:
  - Enhanced mode: Show the edited ProseMirror text
  - Original mode: Show cropped PDF image

  // Toggle region display
  function toggleRegion(regionId, mode) {
    const regionElement = document.querySelector(`region[id="${regionId}"]`);

    if (mode === 'original') {
      // Replace text with PDF crop
      regionElement.innerHTML = `<img src="${getPDFCrop(regionId)}" />`;
    } else {
      // Show edited text
      regionElement.innerHTML = getEditedText(regionId);
    }
  }

  This approach gives you a truly living document where:
  - The text is fully editable and structured
  - Every piece of text maintains its connection to the source PDF
  - You can seamlessly switch between enhanced and original views
  - The document can evolve beyond just transcription into rich, structured content

  This feels like the ideal synthesis of your workflow! Does this capture what you were envisioning?

     # Chaya Document Processing Workflow
     ## Overview
     Chaya is a comprehensive document digitization pipeline that transforms PDFs into rich, structured digital documents. It uses a three-tab workflow (Mark, Edit, Read) where users progressively enhance documents while maintaining full
     bidirectional traceability between original and processed content.
     ## Core Philosophy
     ### **Progressive Enhancement**
     - Start with raw PDF
     - Add spatial structure (regions)

        523 +      // Helper functions for annotation management
        524 +      function setupAnnotationDrawing(overlay: HTMLDivElement, pageDiv: HTMLDivElement, pageNumber: number) {
        525 +          overlay.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
        526 +              // Only start drawing on left mouse button (button 0)
        527 +              if (e.button !== 0) return;
        528 +  
        529 +              isDrawing = true;
        530 +              const rect = overlay.getBoundingClientRect();
        531 +              startX = e.clientX - rect.left;
        532 +              startY = e.clientY - rect.top;
        533 +  
        534 +              // Create a new temporary annotation box
        535 +              currentAnnotation = document.createElement('div');
        536 +              currentAnnotation.className = 'annotation-box-tmp';
        537 +              currentAnnotation.style.position = 'absolute';
        538 +              currentAnnotation.style.border = '2px solid #ff0000';
        539 +              currentAnnotation.style.backgroundColor = 'rgba(255, 0, 0, 0.1)';
        540 +              currentAnnotation.style.left = `${startX}px`;
        541 +              currentAnnotation.style.top = `${startY}px`;
        542 +              currentAnnotation.style.width = '0px';
        543 +              currentAnnotation.style.height = '0px';
        544 +              currentAnnotation.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
        545 +  
        546 +              overlay.appendChild(currentAnnotation);
        547 +              e.preventDefault();
        548 +          });
        549 +  
        550 +          overlay.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
        551 +              if (!isDrawing || !currentAnnotation) return;
        552 +  
        553 +              const rect = overlay.getBoundingClientRect();
        554 +              const currentX = e.clientX - rect.left;
        555 +              const currentY = e.clientY - rect.top;
        556 +  
        557 +              const width = Math.abs(currentX - startX);
        558 +              const height = Math.abs(currentY - startY);
        559 +              const left = Math.min(startX, currentX);
        560 +              const top = Math.min(startY, currentY);
        561 +  
        562 +              currentAnnotation.style.left = `${left}px`;
        563 +              currentAnnotation.style.top = `${top}px`;
        564 +              currentAnnotation.style.width = `${width}px`;
        565 +              currentAnnotation.style.height = `${height}px`;
        566 +          });
        567 +  
        568 +          overlay.addEventListener('mouseup', (e) => {
        569 +              if (!isDrawing || !currentAnnotation) return;
        570 +  
        571 +              isDrawing = false;
        572 +              const rect = overlay.getBoundingClientRect();
        573 +              const endX = e.clientX - rect.left;
        574 +              const endY = e.clientY - rect.top;
        575 +  
        576 +              const width = Math.abs(endX - startX);
        577 +              const height = Math.abs(endY - startY);
        578 +  
        579 +              // Only create annotation if it has meaningful size
        580 +              if (width > 5 && height > 5) {
        581 +                  const left = Math.min(startX, endX);
        582 +                  const top = Math.min(startY, endY);
        583 +  
        584 +                  // Convert to fractional coordinates
        585 +                  const pageWidth = pageDiv.offsetWidth;
        586 +                  const pageHeight = pageDiv.offsetHeight;
        587 +  
        588 +                  const annotation: Annotation = {
        589 +                      id: generateId(),
        590 +                      x: left / pageWidth,
        591 +                      y: top / pageHeight,
        592 +                      width: width / pageWidth,
        593 +                      height: height / pageHeight,
        594 +                      label: prompt('Enter label for this annotation:') || 'Unlabeled',
        595 +                      pageNumber: pageNumber
        596 +                  };
        597 +  
        598 +                  annotations.push(annotation);
        599 +                  hasUnsavedChanges = true;
        600 +  
        601 +                  // Remove the temporary annotation
        602 +                  overlay.removeChild(currentAnnotation);
        603 +                  
        604 +                  // Create proper annotation box using the same path as loaded annotations
        605 +                  createAnnotationBox(overlay, pageDiv, annotation);
        606 +                  
        607 +                  // Auto-select the newly created annotation so user can resize it immediately
        608 +                  const newAnnotationBox = overlay.querySelector(`[data-annotation-id="${annotation.id}"]`) as HTMLDivElement;
        609 +                  if (newAnnotationBox) {
        610 +                      selectAnnotation(newAnnotationBox, annotation);
        611 +                  }
        612 +  
        613 +                  // Update the annotation list
        614 +                  updateAnnotationList();
        615 +  
        616 +                  console.log('Created annotation:', annotation);
        617 +              } else {
        618 +                  // Remove the annotation if it's too small
        619 +                  overlay.removeChild(currentAnnotation);
        620 +              }
        621 +  
        622 +              currentAnnotation = null;
        623 +          });
        624 +      }
        625 +  
        626 +      function createAnnotationBox(overlay: HTMLDivElement, pageDiv: HTMLDivElement, annotation: Annotation): void {
        627 +          // Check if annotation box already exists to prevent duplicates
        628 +          const existingBox = overlay.querySelector(`[data-annotation-id="${annotation.id}"]`);
        629 +          if (existingBox) {
        630 +              console.log(`Annotation box with ID ${annotation.id} already exists, skipping creation`);
        631 +              return;
        632 +          }
        633 +  
        634 +          const pageWidth = pageDiv.offsetWidth;
        635 +          const pageHeight = pageDiv.offsetHeight;
        636 +  
        637 +          // Convert fractional coordinates back to pixels
        638 +          const left = annotation.x * pageWidth;
        639 +          const top = annotation.y * pageHeight;
        640 +          const width = annotation.width * pageWidth;
        641 +          const height = annotation.height * pageHeight;
        642 +  
        643 +          const annotationBox = document.createElement('div');
        644 +          annotationBox.className = 'annotation-box';
        645 +          annotationBox.style.position = 'absolute';
        646 +          annotationBox.style.border = '2px solid #ff0000';
        647 +          annotationBox.style.backgroundColor = 'rgba(255, 0, 0, 0.1)';
        648 +          annotationBox.style.left = `${left}px`;
        649 +          annotationBox.style.top = `${top}px`;
        650 +          annotationBox.style.width = `${width}px`;
        651 +          annotationBox.style.height = `${height}px`;
        652 +          annotationBox.style.cursor = 'move';
        653 +          annotationBox.title = annotation.label;
        654 +          annotationBox.dataset.annotationId = annotation.id;
        655 +  
        656 +          // Make annotation interactive
        657 +          makeAnnotationInteractive(annotationBox, annotation, pageDiv);
        658 +  
        659 +          overlay.appendChild(annotationBox);
        660 +      }
        661 +  
        662 +      function makeAnnotationInteractive(annotationBox: HTMLDivElement, annotation: Annotation, pageDiv: HTMLDivElement): void {
        663 +          // Add resize handles
        664 +          const handles = ['nw', 'ne', 'sw', 'se', 'n', 's', 'e', 'w'];
        665 +          handles.forEach(handle => {
        666 +              const handleElement = document.createElement('div');
        667 +              handleElement.className = `resize-handle resize-${handle}`;
        668 +              handleElement.style.position = 'absolute';
        669 +              handleElement.style.backgroundColor = '#fff';
        670 +              handleElement.style.border = '1px solid #000';
        671 +              handleElement.style.width = '8px';
        672 +              handleElement.style.height = '8px';
        673 +              handleElement.style.zIndex = '1000';
        674 +  
        675 +              // Position handles
        676 +              switch (handle) {
        677 +                  case 'nw':
        678 +                      handleElement.style.top = '-4px';
        679 +                      handleElement.style.left = '-4px';
        680 +                      handleElement.style.cursor = 'nw-resize';
        681 +                      break;
        682 +                  case 'ne':
        683 +                      handleElement.style.top = '-4px';
        684 +                      handleElement.style.right = '-4px';
        685 +                      handleElement.style.cursor = 'ne-resize';
        686 +                      break;
        687 +                  case 'sw':
        688 +                      handleElement.style.bottom = '-4px';
        689 +                      handleElement.style.left = '-4px';
        690 +                      handleElement.style.cursor = 'sw-resize';
        691 +                      break;
        692 +                  case 'se':
        693 +                      handleElement.style.bottom = '-4px';
        694 +                      handleElement.style.right = '-4px';
        695 +                      handleElement.style.cursor = 'se-resize';
        696 +                      break;
        697 +                  case 'n':
        698 +                      handleElement.style.top = '-4px';
        699 +                      handleElement.style.left = '50%';
        700 +                      handleElement.style.transform = 'translateX(-50%)';
        701 +                      handleElement.style.cursor = 'n-resize';
        702 +                      break;
        703 +                  case 's':
        704 +                      handleElement.style.bottom = '-4px';
        705 +                      handleElement.style.left = '50%';
        706 +                      handleElement.style.transform = 'translateX(-50%)';
        707 +                      handleElement.style.cursor = 's-resize';
        708 +                      break;
        709 +                  case 'e':
        710 +                      handleElement.style.right = '-4px';
        711 +                      handleElement.style.top = '50%';
        712 +                      handleElement.style.transform = 'translateY(-50%)';
        713 +                      handleElement.style.cursor = 'e-resize';
        714 +                      break;
        715 +                  case 'w':
        716 +                      handleElement.style.left = '-4px';
        717 +                      handleElement.style.top = '50%';
        718 +                      handleElement.style.transform = 'translateY(-50%)';
        719 +                      handleElement.style.cursor = 'w-resize';
        720 +                      break;
        721 +              }
        722 +  
        723 +              // Initially hide handles
        724 +              handleElement.style.display = 'none';
        725 +  
        726 +              // Add resize functionality
        727 +              handleElement.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
        728 +                  e.stopPropagation();
        729 +                  startResize(e, handle, annotationBox, annotation, pageDiv);
        730 +              });
        731 +  
        732 +              annotationBox.appendChild(handleElement);
        733 +          });

        740 +                  const newLabel = window.prompt('Edit label:', annotation.label);

        742 +                      annotation.label = newLabel.trim();

        771 +      function selectAnnotation(annotationBox: HTMLDivElement, annotation: Annotation): void {
        772 +          // Hide handles from previously selected annotation
        773 +          if (selectedAnnotation && selectedAnnotation !== annotationBox) {
        774 +              hideResizeHandles(selectedAnnotation);
        775 +              selectedAnnotation.style.border = '2px solid #ff0000';
        776 +          }
        777 +  
        778 +          // Select new annotation
        779 +          selectedAnnotation = annotationBox;
        780 +          selectedAnnotationData = annotation;
        781 +          annotationBox.style.border = '2px solid #0066ff';
        782 +          showResizeHandles(annotationBox);
        783 +      }
        784 +  
        785 +      function showResizeHandles(annotationBox: HTMLDivElement): void {
        786 +          const handles = annotationBox.querySelectorAll('.resize-handle');
        787 +          handles.forEach(handle => {
        788 +              (handle as HTMLElement).style.display = 'block';
        789 +          });
        790 +      }
        791 +  
        792 +      function hideResizeHandles(annotationBox: HTMLDivElement): void {
        793 +          const handles = annotationBox.querySelectorAll('.resize-handle');
        794 +          handles.forEach(handle => {
        795 +              (handle as HTMLElement).style.display = 'none';
        796 +          });
        797 +      }
        798 +  
        799 +      function startResize(e: MouseEvent, handle: string, annotationBox: HTMLDivElement, annotation: Annotation, pageDiv: HTMLDivElement): void {
        800 +          isResizing = true;
        801 +          resizeHandle = handle;
        802 +          startX = e.clientX;
        803 +          startY = e.clientY;
        804 +  
        805 +          const rect = annotationBox.getBoundingClientRect();
        806 +          const startWidth = rect.width;
        807 +          const startHeight = rect.height;
        808 +          const startLeft = parseFloat(annotationBox.style.left);
        809 +          const startTop = parseFloat(annotationBox.style.top);
        810 +  
        811 +          const handleMouseMove = (e: MouseEvent) => {
        812 +              if (!isResizing) return;
        813 +  
        814 +              const deltaX = e.clientX - startX;
        815 +              const deltaY = e.clientY - startY;
        816 +  
        817 +              let newLeft = startLeft;
        818 +              let newTop = startTop;
        819 +              let newWidth = startWidth;
        820 +              let newHeight = startHeight;
        821 +  
        822 +              switch (resizeHandle) {
        823 +                  case 'nw':
        824 +                      newLeft = startLeft + deltaX;
        825 +                      newTop = startTop + deltaY;
        826 +                      newWidth = startWidth - deltaX;
        827 +                      newHeight = startHeight - deltaY;
        828 +                      break;
        829 +                  case 'ne':
        830 +                      newTop = startTop + deltaY;
        831 +                      newWidth = startWidth + deltaX;
        832 +                      newHeight = startHeight - deltaY;
        833 +                      break;
        834 +                  case 'sw':
        835 +                      newLeft = startLeft + deltaX;
        836 +                      newWidth = startWidth - deltaX;
        837 +                      newHeight = startHeight + deltaY;
        838 +                      break;
        839 +                  case 'se':
        840 +                      newWidth = startWidth + deltaX;
        841 +                      newHeight = startHeight + deltaY;
        842 +                      break;
        843 +                  case 'n':
        844 +                      newTop = startTop + deltaY;
        845 +                      newHeight = startHeight - deltaY;
        846 +                      break;
        847 +                  case 's':
        848 +                      newHeight = startHeight + deltaY;
        849 +                      break;
        850 +                  case 'e':
        851 +                      newWidth = startWidth + deltaX;
        852 +                      break;
        853 +                  case 'w':
        854 +                      newLeft = startLeft + deltaX;
        855 +                      newWidth = startWidth - deltaX;
        856 +                      break;
        857 +              }
        858 +  
        859 +              // Ensure minimum size
        860 +              if (newWidth < 10) newWidth = 10;
        861 +              if (newHeight < 10) newHeight = 10;
        862 +  
        863 +              // Apply changes
        864 +              annotationBox.style.left = `${newLeft}px`;
        865 +              annotationBox.style.top = `${newTop}px`;
        866 +              annotationBox.style.width = `${newWidth}px`;
        867 +              annotationBox.style.height = `${newHeight}px`;
        868 +  
        869 +              // Update annotation data with fractional coordinates
        870 +              const pageWidth = pageDiv.offsetWidth;
        871 +              const pageHeight = pageDiv.offsetHeight;
        872 +  
        873 +              annotation.x = newLeft / pageWidth;
        874 +              annotation.y = newTop / pageHeight;
        875 +              annotation.width = newWidth / pageWidth;
        876 +              annotation.height = newHeight / pageHeight;
        877 +              hasUnsavedChanges = true;
        878 +          };
        879 +  
        880 +          const handleMouseUp = () => {
        881 +              isResizing = false;
        882 +              resizeHandle = null;
        883 +              document.removeEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
        884 +              document.removeEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
        885 +          };
        886 +  
        887 +          document.addEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
        888 +          document.addEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
        889 +          e.preventDefault();
        890 +      }
        891 +  
        892 +      function startDrag(e: MouseEvent, annotationBox: HTMLDivElement, annotation: Annotation, pageDiv: HTMLDivElement): void {
        893 +          isDragging = true;
        894 +          dragStartX = e.clientX;
        895 +          dragStartY = e.clientY;
        896 +  
        897 +          const startLeft = parseFloat(annotationBox.style.left);
        898 +          const startTop = parseFloat(annotationBox.style.top);
        899 +  
        900 +          const handleMouseMove = (e: MouseEvent) => {
        901 +              if (!isDragging) return;
        902 +  
        903 +              const deltaX = e.clientX - dragStartX;
        904 +              const deltaY = e.clientY - dragStartY;
        905 +  
        906 +              const newLeft = startLeft + deltaX;
        907 +              const newTop = startTop + deltaY;
        908 +  
        909 +              // Keep annotation within page bounds
        910 +              const pageWidth = pageDiv.offsetWidth;
        911 +              const pageHeight = pageDiv.offsetHeight;
        912 +              const boxWidth = parseFloat(annotationBox.style.width);
        913 +              const boxHeight = parseFloat(annotationBox.style.height);
        914 +  
        915 +              const clampedLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(newLeft, pageWidth - boxWidth));
        916 +              const clampedTop = Math.max(0, Math.min(newTop, pageHeight - boxHeight));
        917 +  
        918 +              annotationBox.style.left = `${clampedLeft}px`;
        919 +              annotationBox.style.top = `${clampedTop}px`;
        920 +  
        921 +              // Update annotation data with fractional coordinates
        922 +              annotation.x = clampedLeft / pageWidth;
        923 +              annotation.y = clampedTop / pageHeight;
        924 +              hasUnsavedChanges = true;
        925 +          };
        926 +  
        927 +          const handleMouseUp = () => {
        928 +              isDragging = false;
        929 +              document.removeEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
        930 +              document.removeEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
        931 +          };
        932 +  
        933 +          document.addEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
        934 +          document.addEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
        935 +          e.preventDefault();
        936 +      }
        937 +  
        938 +      function selectAnnotationById(annotationId: string): void {
        939 +          const annotation = annotations.find(a => a.id === annotationId);
        940 +          if (!annotation) return;
        941 +  
        942 +          const annotationBox = document.querySelector(`.annotation-box[data-annotation-id="${annotationId}"]`) as HTMLDivElement;
        943 +          if (annotationBox) {
        944 +              selectAnnotation(annotationBox, annotation);
        945 +              annotationBox.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
        946 +          }
        947 +      }
        948 +  
        949 +      function highlightAnnotationById(annotationId: string, highlight: boolean): void {
        950 +          const annotationBox = document.querySelector(`.annotation-box[data-annotation-id="${annotationId}"]`) as HTMLDivElement;
        951 +          if (annotationBox) {
        952 +              if (highlight) {
        953 +                  annotationBox.style.boxShadow = '0 0 0 3px rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.5)';
        954 +              } else {
        955 +                  annotationBox.style.boxShadow = '';
        956 +              }
        957 +          }
        958 +      }
        959 +  
        960 +      function highlightSidebarAnnotation(annotationId: string, highlight: boolean): void {
        961 +          const sidebarItem = document.querySelector(`#annotation-list [data-annotation-id="${annotationId}"]`) as HTMLDivElement;
        962 +          if (sidebarItem) {
        963 +              if (highlight) {
        964 +                  sidebarItem.style.backgroundColor = '#dbeafe';
        965 +                  sidebarItem.style.transform = 'scale(1.02)';
        966 +                  sidebarItem.style.transition = 'all 0.2s ease';
        967 +              } else {
        968 +                  sidebarItem.style.backgroundColor = '';
        969 +                  sidebarItem.style.transform = '';
        970 +              }
        971 +          }
        972 +      }
        973 +  
        974 +      function scrollToSidebarAnnotation(annotationId: string): void {
        975 +          const sidebarItem = document.querySelector(`#annotation-list [data-annotation-id="${annotationId}"]`) as HTMLDivElement;
        976 +          if (sidebarItem) {
        977 +              sidebarItem.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
        978 +              highlightSidebarAnnotation(annotationId, true);
        979 +              setTimeout(() => highlightSidebarAnnotation(annotationId, false), 2000);
        980 +          }
        981 +      }
        982 +  
        983 +      function deleteAnnotationById(annotationId: string): void {
        984 +          const index = annotations.findIndex(a => a.id === annotationId);
        985 +          if (index === -1) return;
        986 +  
        987 +          annotations.splice(index, 1);
        988 +          hasUnsavedChanges = true;
        989 +  
        990 +          const annotationBoxes = document.querySelectorAll(`.annotation-box[data-annotation-id="${annotationId}"]`);
        991 +          annotationBoxes.forEach(box => {
        992 +              box.remove();
        993 +          });
        994 +  
        995 +          updateAnnotationList();
        996 +  
        997 +          if (selectedAnnotationData && selectedAnnotationData.id === annotationId) {
        998 +              selectedAnnotation = null;
        999 +              selectedAnnotationData = null;
       1000 +          }
       1001 +      }
       1002 +  
       1003 +      function getCanvasForPage(pageNumber: number): HTMLCanvasElement | null {
       1004 +          const pageDiv = pdfContainer.querySelector(`[data-page-number="${pageNumber}"]`) as HTMLDivElement;
       1005 +          if (pageDiv) {
       1006 +              return pageDiv.querySelector('canvas');
       1007 +          }
       1008 +          return null;
       1009 +      }
       1010 +  
       1011 +      // Clean up temporary annotations on any click and handle deselection
       1012 +      document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
       1013 +          const tempAnnotations = document.querySelectorAll('.annotation-box-tmp');
       1014 +          tempAnnotations.forEach(temp => temp.remove());
       1015 +  
       1016 +          isDrawing = false;
       1017 +          currentAnnotation = null;
       1018 +  
       1019 +          if (selectedAnnotation && !selectedAnnotation.contains(e.target as Node)) {
       1020 +              hideResizeHandles(selectedAnnotation);
       1021 +              selectedAnnotation.style.border = '2px solid #ff0000';
       1022 +              selectedAnnotation = null;
       1023 +              selectedAnnotationData = null;
       1024 +          }
       1025 +      });
       1026 +  
       1027 +      // Event listener for delete annotation buttons
       1028 +      document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
       1029 +          const deleteBtn = (e.target as Element).closest('.delete-annotation-btn') as HTMLElement;
       1030 +          if (deleteBtn) {
       1031 +              e.stopPropagation();
       1032 +              const annotationId = deleteBtn.dataset.annotationId;
       1033 +              if (annotationId) {
       1034 +                  deleteAnnotationById(annotationId);
       1035 +              }
       1036 +          }
       1037 +      });
       1038 +  
       1039 +      // Warn user about unsaved changes when leaving the page
       1040 +      window.addEventListener('beforeunload', (e) => {
       1041 +          if (hasUnsavedChanges && annotations.length > 0) {
       1042 +              const message = 'You have unsaved annotations. Are you sure you want to leave?';
       1043 +              e.preventDefault();
       1044 +              e.returnValue = message;
       1045 +              return message;
       1046 +          }
       1047 +      });

       129 +      private updateFileStatus(): void {

       149 +      private async loadFiles(): Promise<void> {
       150 +          if (!this.state.pdfFile) return;
       151 +  
       152 +          const loadingDiv = document.getElementById('app-loading') as HTMLDivElement;
       153 +          const loadingText = document.getElementById('app-loading-text') as HTMLSpanElement;
       154 +          const loadingPercent = document.getElementById('app-loading-percent') as HTMLSpanElement;
       155 +          const progressBar = document.getElementById('app-progress-bar') as HTMLDivElement;
       156 +          const loadingDetails = document.getElementById('app-loading-details') as HTMLDivElement;
       157 +  
       158 +          try {
       159 +              // Show loading progress
       160 +              loadingDiv.classList.remove('hidden');
       161 +              this.updateLoadingProgress(0, 'Loading PDF...', 'Reading PDF file...');
       162 +  
       163 +              // Load PDF
       164 +              const pdfArrayBuffer = await this.readFileAsArrayBuffer(this.state.pdfFile);
       165 +              this.updateLoadingProgress(30, 'Processing PDF...', 'Initializing PDF.js...');
       166 +  
       167 +              const pdfjs = await waitForPdfjs();
       168 +              const loadingTask = pdfjs.getDocument(new Uint8Array(pdfArrayBuffer));
       169 +              this.state.pdfDocument = await loadingTask.promise;
       170 +  
       171 +              this.updateLoadingProgress(70, 'Loading annotations...', 'Processing annotation file...');
       172 +  
       173 +              // Load annotations if provided
       174 +              if (this.state.annotationsFile) {
       175 +                  const annotationsText = await this.readFileAsText(this.state.annotationsFile);
       176 +                  const annotationsData = JSON.parse(annotationsText);
       177 +                  this.state.loadedAnnotations = parseAnnotationsFromJson(annotationsData);
       178 +              } else {
       179 +                  this.state.loadedAnnotations = [];
       180 +                  this.state.loadedAnnotationsFileName = null;
       181 +              }
       182 +  
       183 +              this.updateLoadingProgress(100, 'Complete!', 'Files loaded successfully');
       184 +              
       185 +              // Mark as loaded
       186 +              this.state.documentLoaded = true;
       187 +              this.state.hasUnsavedChanges = false;
       188 +  
       189 +              // Notify tabs that data is ready
       190 +              this.notifyTabsDataReady();
       191 +  
       192 +              // Hide loading after a short delay
       193 +              setTimeout(() => {
       194 +                  loadingDiv.classList.add('hidden');
       195 +              }, 1000);
       196 +  
       197 +          } catch (error) {
       198 +              console.error('Error loading files:', error);
       199 +              this.updateLoadingProgress(0, 'Error loading files', `Failed: ${error}`);
       200 +              setTimeout(() => {
       201 +                  loadingDiv.classList.add('hidden');
       202 +              }, 3000);
       203 +          }
       204 +      }
       205 +  
       206 +      private updateLoadingProgress(percent: number, text: string, details: string): void {
       207 +          const loadingText = document.getElementById('app-loading-text') as HTMLSpanElement;
       208 +          const loadingPercent = document.getElementById('app-loading-percent') as HTMLSpanElement;
       209 +          const progressBar = document.getElementById('app-progress-bar') as HTMLDivElement;
       210 +          const loadingDetails = document.getElementById('app-loading-details') as HTMLDivElement;
       211 +  
       212 +          const clampedPercent = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, percent));
       213 +          
       214 +          progressBar.style.width = `${clampedPercent}%`;
       215 +          loadingPercent.textContent = `${Math.round(clampedPercent)}%`;
       216 +          loadingText.textContent = text;
       217 +          loadingDetails.textContent = details;
       218 +          
       219 +          // Update progress bar color based on status
       220 +          if (clampedPercent === 100) {
       221 +              progressBar.className = 'bg-green-600 h-2 rounded-full transition-all duration-300';
       222 +          } else if (clampedPercent === 0 && text.includes('Error')) {
       223 +              progressBar.className = 'bg-red-600 h-2 rounded-full transition-all duration-300';
       224 +          } else {
       225 +              progressBar.className = 'bg-blue-600 h-2 rounded-full transition-all duration-300';
       226 +          }
       227 +      }
       228 +  
       229 +      private readFileAsArrayBuffer(file: File): Promise<ArrayBuffer> {
       230 +          return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
       231 +              const reader = new FileReader();
       232 +              reader.onload = (e) => resolve(e.target?.result as ArrayBuffer);
       233 +              reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error);
       234 +              reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
       235 +          });
       236 +      }
       237 +  
       238 +      private readFileAsText(file: File): Promise<string> {
       239 +          return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
       240 +              const reader = new FileReader();
       241 +              reader.onload = (e) => resolve(e.target?.result as string);
       242 +              reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error);
       243 +              reader.readAsText(file);
       244 +          });
       245 +      }
       246 +  
       247 +      private notifyTabsDataReady(): void {
       248 +          // Dispatch custom events to notify tabs that data is ready
       249 +          const dataReadyEvent = new CustomEvent('appDataReady', {
       250 +              detail: {
       251 +                  pdfDocument: this.state.pdfDocument,
       252 +                  annotations: this.state.loadedAnnotations,
       253 +                  annotationsFileName: this.state.loadedAnnotationsFileName,
       254 +                  pdfFileName: this.state.pdfFile?.name
       255 +              }
       256 +          });
       257 +          
       258 +          document.dispatchEvent(dataReadyEvent);
       259 +      }
       260 +  
       261 +      // Public methods for tabs to access shared state
       262 +      public getSharedState() {

       265 +              annotations: this.state.loadedAnnotations,
       266 +              annotationsFileName: this.state.loadedAnnotationsFileName,
       267 +              pdfFileName: this.state.pdfFile?.name,
       268 +              documentLoaded: this.state.documentLoaded
       269 +          };
       270 +      }

⏺ Perfect! I have successfully implemented centralized file loading for the unified three-tab application. Here's what was accomplished:

  ✅ Centralized File Loading Implementation Complete

  What was implemented:

  1. ✅ Centralized File Loading UI
    - Moved PDF and annotations file selection to the app header
    - Single "Load Files" button that processes both files
    - Unified progress bar and status display
    - Better user experience with clear status messages
  2. ✅ Shared State Management
    - Enhanced ChayaApp class with comprehensive state management
    - PDF document, annotations, and file metadata stored centrally
    - Unsaved changes tracking across tabs
    - Public methods for tabs to access and update shared state
  3. ✅ Event-Driven Architecture
    - Custom appDataReady event to notify tabs when data is ready
    - Clean separation between centralized loading and tab-specific logic
    - Automatic synchronization between tabs and app state
  4. ✅ Mark Tab Integration
    - Removed duplicate file loading UI and logic
    - Connected to centralized data via event listeners
    - All annotation operations sync with app state
    - Save functionality uses centralized file information
  5. ✅ Read Tab Integration
    - Simplified to focus on presentation functionality
    - Automatically displays regions when centralized data is ready
    - No duplicate file loading or processing

  Key Benefits:

  - Single Source of Truth: All file loading happens once at the app level
  - Better UX: Users load files once and can switch between tabs seamlessly
  - Reduced Redundancy: No duplicate file loading logic or UI
  - Consistent State: All tabs work with the same data
  - Improved Performance: Files are loaded once, not per tab

  Current Application Flow:

  1. Load Files: User selects PDF and optional annotations file at the app level
  2. Processing: Centralized loading with progress indication
  3. Data Ready: All tabs are notified when data is available
  4. Tab Switching: Users can seamlessly switch between Mark and Read tabs
  5. State Sync: Changes in Mark tab are automatically available in Read tab

       ```typescript
       private notifyTabsDataReady(): void {
           const dataReadyEvent = new CustomEvent('appDataReady', {
               detail: {
                   pdfDocument: this.state.pdfDocument,
                   annotations: this.state.loadedAnnotations,
                   annotationsFileName: this.state.loadedAnnotationsFileName,
                   pdfFileName: this.state.pdfFile?.name
               }
           });
           document.dispatchEvent(dataReadyEvent);
       }
       ```

       - Updated to use centralized data via event listeners
       - Removed duplicate file loading logic
       - Added syncWithAppState() function to keep app state updated
       ```typescript
       document.addEventListener('appDataReady', (event: Event) => {
           const customEvent = event as CustomEvent;
           const { pdfDocument, annotations: loadedAnnotations, annotationsFileName, pdfFileName } = customEvent.detail;
           handleDataReady(pdfDocument, loadedAnnotations, annotationsFileName, pdfFileName);
       });
       ```
     
     - **src/modes/viewer.ts**
       - Simplified to focus on presentation functionality
       - Removed duplicate file loading, now uses centralized data
       - Automatically displays regions when data is ready
       ```typescript
       function handleDataReady(pdfDocument: any, annotations: Annotation[], annotationsFileName: string | null, pdfFileName: string | null): void {
           loadedAnnotations = annotations || [];
           if (!pdfDocument || !annotations || annotations.length === 0) {
               // Show placeholder message
               return;
           }
           displayAnnotatedRegions(pdfDocument, annotations);
       }
       ```

> Why were those classes not being generated by Tailwind? Why were some classes not available?


⏺ Great question! The issue was with Tailwind's content purging system. Here's what happened:

  How Tailwind CSS Works

  Tailwind CSS uses a "purge" or "content" system that:

  1. Scans your files for class names
  2. Only generates CSS for classes it finds in use
  3. Removes unused classes to keep the final CSS file small

  The Problem

  When I added new classes to index.html, Tailwind's build process didn't detect them because:

  - ✅ ./viewer.html (old file)
  - ❌ ./index.html (new file) - MISSING!

⏺ Summary: Why Classes Weren't Generated

  The root cause was a configuration issue, not a CSS problem:

  1. Tailwind's content scanning: Tailwind only generates CSS for classes it finds in the files specified in tailwind.config.js
  2. Outdated configuration: The config was pointing to old files (annotator.html, viewer.html) but missing the new index.html
  3. Missing classes: Any classes used in index.html weren't being detected, so Tailwind didn't generate them
  4. The fix: Added "./index.html" to the content array in tailwind.config.js

  This is a common issue when:
  - Refactoring file structures
  - Adding new HTML files
  - Moving from multiple files to a unified structure
  - Forgetting to update build tool configurations

  The CSS itself was never "wonky" - it was just incomplete due to the build configuration not scanning the right files!

⏺ Perfect! Now I can see the key difference that explains why PDF loading is much faster now:

  Key Performance Improvements

  1. Centralized Loading vs Per-Tab Loading

  Old Architecture (slow):
  // Each tab had its own file input and loading process
  pdfUpload.addEventListener('change', async (event) => {
      // PDF loads and renders immediately on file selection
      const fileReader = new FileReader();
      fileReader.onload = async (e) => {
          const typedArray = new Uint8Array(e.target?.result as ArrayBuffer);
          const pdfjs = await waitForPdfjs();
          const loadingTask = pdfjs.getDocument(typedArray);
          const pdf = await loadingTask.promise;

          // Render ALL pages immediately
          for (let i = 1; i <= pdf.numPages; i++) {
              await renderPage(pdf, i, containerWidth);
          }
      };
  });

  New Architecture (fast):
  // Centralized loading - PDF processed once, then shared
  private async loadFiles(): Promise<void> {
      const pdfArrayBuffer = await this.readFileAsArrayBuffer(this.state.pdfFile);
      const pdfjs = await waitForPdfjs();
      const loadingTask = pdfjs.getDocument(new Uint8Array(pdfArrayBuffer));
      this.state.pdfDocument = await loadingTask.promise;

      // Only notify tabs when ready - no immediate rendering
      this.notifyTabsDataReady();
  }

  2. Load vs Render Separation

  Old: PDF loading and rendering happened together, blocking the UI
  New: PDF is loaded and parsed first, then tabs receive the pre-processed document

  3. Event-Driven Architecture

  Old: Direct file input → immediate processing → immediate rendering
  New: File selection → user clicks "Load" → background processing → notify tabs when ready

  4. Reduced File I/O

  Old: Each tab might reload the same PDF file
  New: PDF loaded once, shared across all tabs

  5. Better Progress Feedback

  Old: Basic progress bar during rendering
  New: Detailed progress tracking (reading → processing → annotations → complete)

  Why It's Faster

  1. Deferred Rendering: The PDF is loaded and parsed, but pages aren't rendered until needed
  2. Shared State: No duplicate loading between tabs
  3. Explicit User Action: User must click "Load Files" button, so they expect the loading time
  4. Background Processing: PDF processing happens in app.ts while tabs wait for the ready event
  5. Optimized Flow: Load → Parse → Notify vs Load → Parse → Render → Show

  The old code was doing immediate rendering on file selection, while the new code does deferred rendering on data ready events. This separation of concerns makes the perceived performance much better!


> What does this mean? Are the pages still rendered one at a time? Could they show up out of order?


⏺ Great question! Let me look at how the pages are actually rendered in the new system:


⏺ Read(src/modes/annotator.ts)

  ⎿  Read 30 lines (ctrl+r to expand)


⏺ You're absolutely right to question this! Looking at the code:

  async function renderPdfPages(pdfDocument: any, container: HTMLDivElement): Promise<void> {
      const containerWidth = container.offsetWidth;

      for (let i = 1; i <= pdfDocument.numPages; i++) {
          await renderPage(pdfDocument, i, containerWidth);  // ← Still sequential!
      }
  }

  What Actually Happens

  Pages are still rendered one at a time, in order - the await keyword means each page fully renders before the next one starts. So:

  1. ✅ Pages render in order (1, 2, 3, 4...)
  2. ❌ Pages cannot show up out of order
  3. ❌ Pages are not rendered in parallel

  The Performance Improvement Was Different

  The speed improvement wasn't from parallel rendering - it was from separating the loading phases:

  Old Flow:
  File selected → Read file → Parse PDF → Render page 1 → Render page 2 → ...
                ↑_________________________________ User waits here

  New Flow:
  File selected → Read file → Parse PDF → Notify tabs → Render page 1 → Render page 2 → ...
                ↑_____________________ User waits    ↑_____________ User sees progress

  The key insight is that PDF parsing (which is heavy) now happens in the background before rendering starts, rather than being interleaved with rendering.

  Could We Make It Even Faster?

  Yes! We could render pages in parallel:

  async function renderPdfPages(pdfDocument: any, container: HTMLDivElement): Promise<void> {
      const containerWidth = container.offsetWidth;

      // Render all pages in parallel
      const renderPromises = [];
      for (let i = 1; i <= pdfDocument.numPages; i++) {
          renderPromises.push(renderPage(pdfDocument, i, containerWidth));
      }

      await Promise.all(renderPromises); // Wait for all to complete
  }

  But this would mean:
  - ✅ Much faster overall rendering
  - ❌ Pages could appear out of order
  - ❌ Higher memory usage
  - ❌ More complex DOM management (need to ensure proper ordering)

  The current approach prioritizes predictable UX over maximum speed.

  So What Actually Changed?

⏺ Found It! The Progress Bar Timing Has Completely Changed

  Old System (slower progress):
  // Progress goes from 40% to 90% during page rendering
  for (let i = 1; i <= pdf.numPages; i++) {
      const pageProgress = 40 + (50 * i / pdf.numPages);  // 40% → 90%
      updateLoadingProgress(pageProgress, `Rendering page ${i} of ${pdf.numPages}...`);
      await renderPage(pdf, i, containerWidth);  // ← RENDER HAPPENS HERE
  }
  updateLoadingProgress(100, 'Complete!', 'PDF ready for annotation');  // ← 100% AFTER rendering

  New System (faster progress):
  // Progress hits 100% BEFORE any pages are rendered
  this.updateLoadingProgress(100, 'Complete!', 'Files loaded successfully');  // ← 100% BEFORE rendering
  this.notifyTabsDataReady();  // ← This triggers rendering separately

  What's Actually Happening

  1. Old: Progress bar stays blue and slowly fills up (40% → 90%) while pages render one by one, then turns green at 100%
  2. New: Progress bar quickly hits 100% and turns green after just loading the PDF, then pages render with no progress feedback

  The Illusion of Speed

  The progress bar turns green and disappears much sooner in the new system, creating the perception that everything is faster. But the actual page rendering is happening after the progress bar disappears, so you're not seeing the slow part
   anymore.

  You're not seeing faster rendering - you're seeing the progress bar complete before rendering begins!

  The actual page-by-page rendering time should be identical, but now it happens "invisibly" after the progress completes, making the overall experience feel much snappier.

⏺ Perfect! Now the issue should be fixed. Here's what was happening:

  The Problem

  1. App loads PDF → dispatches appDataReady event
  2. Both annotator AND viewer listen for this event
  3. Annotator starts rendering 279 pages (slow)
  4. Viewer starts processing annotations (fast, especially with no annotations)
  5. Viewer finishes first → dispatches tabRenderingComplete
  6. App receives completion → hides progress bar while annotator is still working

  The Fix

  Now the app only notifies the currently active tab:

  1. App loads PDF → dispatches markTabDataReady (since Mark tab is active)
  2. Only annotator receives the event and starts rendering
  3. Viewer is ignored until the user switches to Read tab
  4. Only annotator can dispatch the completion event
  5. Progress bar stays until all 279 pages are rendered

  When you test again, you should see:
  - Console log: "Notified mark tab that data is ready"
  - No premature hiding of the progress bar
  - Progress bar continues through all 279 pages
  - Only the Mark tab completion event triggers hiding

  This should fix the progress bar disappearing at page 147!

  So it seems the issue was a race condition between when the viewer annotations get extracted, and 1s after that the progress bar goes away.


⏺ Exactly! You've identified the root cause perfectly. The console logs show the race condition clearly:

  08:17:59.261 viewer.js:53 All annotation regions extracted successfully
  08:17:59.262 app.js:207 Rendering complete for read tab: 261 pages

  The viewer was extracting 261 annotation regions and finishing first, then dispatching its completion event, which caused the progress bar to disappear while the annotator was still rendering pages.

  This confirms that both tabs were processing the data simultaneously when appDataReady was dispatched to all listeners.

  With the fix I just implemented:

  1. Before: appDataReady → both annotator AND viewer start working → viewer finishes first → progress bar disappears
  2. After: markTabDataReady → only annotator starts working → only annotator can complete → progress bar waits for all pages

  The fix ensures that:
  - When you're on the Mark tab: only the annotator processes the PDF and renders pages
  - When you switch to Read tab: only then will the viewer process and extract annotation regions
  - No race conditions between tabs fighting over the completion event

  This should completely solve the progress bar disappearing at 50% issue. The progress bar will now accurately reflect the Mark tab's page rendering progress from 30% all the way to 100% without interference from the viewer's annotation
  extraction process.

  Great debugging work tracking down that race condition in the console logs!

  ⎿  Found 1 line (ctrl+r to expand)


⏺ Read(src/modes/annotator.ts)

  ⎿  Read 20 lines (ctrl+r to expand)


⏺ I found the issue! The problem is that the page DOM elements are being appended to the container before the canvas is actually rendered. This means:

  1. Page 1 DOM created → appended to container (shows blank)
  2. Page 2 DOM created → appended to container (shows blank)
  3. Page 1 canvas renders → page 1 shows content
  4. Page 2 canvas renders → page 2 shows content

  But if the canvas rendering happens out of order (due to PDF.js async operations), the visual order gets scrambled.

  Let me fix this by moving the DOM append to after the canvas rendering:


⏺ Update(src/modes/annotator.ts)

  ⎿  Updated src/modes/annotator.ts with 3 additions and 1 removal                 
       307            pageDiv.appendChild(canvas);
       308            pageDiv.appendChild(annotationLayer);

       312                canvasContext: context!,
     ...
       315            };
       316    
       317            await page.render(renderContext).promise;
       318 +          
       319 +          // Only append to container after the page is fully rendered
       320 +          pdfContainer.appendChild(pageDiv);
       321    
       322            // Add mouse event listeners for annotation drawing
       323            setupAnnotationDrawing(annotationLayer, pageDiv, pageNumber);

⏺ Perfect! Now the page rendering order should be correct:

  What Changed

  Before (out of order):
  pdfContainer.appendChild(pageDiv);        // ← Page DOM added immediately (blank)
  await page.render(renderContext).promise; // ← Canvas renders later (async)

  After (correct order):
  await page.render(renderContext).promise; // ← Canvas renders first
  pdfContainer.appendChild(pageDiv);        // ← Page DOM added only after rendering

  Why This Fixes It

  1. Sequential processing: The for loop with await ensures pages are processed 1, 2, 3, 4...
  2. Complete rendering: Each page's canvas is fully rendered before moving to the next
  3. DOM append order: Pages are only added to the container after they're fully rendered
  4. Visual consistency: Pages appear in the correct order: 1, 2, 3, 4...

  The pages should now appear in the correct order from top to bottom, even with a large 279-page PDF. Each page will be fully rendered before being added to the DOM, ensuring the visual order matches the logical page order.

  This approach maintains backward compatibility while introducing the new single-file workflow as a progressive enhancement. The UI clearly communicates the two modes and their benefits without overwhelming users.

  The user is proposing a much simpler UI approach:

  Benefits:

⏺ Yes, this is much cleaner! This approach is elegant and intuitive. Let me visualize how this would work:

  Simplified Two-Slot UI

  Initial State (Empty)

  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ Document Input                                   │
  ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │                                                 │
  │   ┌─────────────┐        ┌─────────────┐        │
  │   │     📦      │        │     📄      │        │
  │   │             │        │             │        │
  │   │ Upload      │   OR   │ Upload      │        │
  │   │ .chaya      │        │ .pdf        │        │
  │   │             │        │             │        │
  │   └─────────────┘        └─────────────┘        │
  │                                                 │
  │ Start with existing work    Start from scratch  │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  After Loading (Active)

  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ Document: research_paper.pdf                    │
  ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │                                                 │
  │   ┌─────────────┐        ┌─────────────┐        │
  │   │     📦      │        │     📄      │        │
  │   │             │        │             │        │
  │   │ Download    │        │ Download    │        │
  │   │ .chaya      │        │ .pdf        │        │
  │   │             │        │             │        │
  │   └─────────────┘        └─────────────┘        │
  │                                                 │
  │ Complete package        Original document       │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  User Mental Model Benefits

  1. Simple: Two formats, two slots, one interaction pattern
  2. Intuitive: Upload to start, download to save
  3. Flexible: Can round-trip between formats easily
  4. Progressive: Start simple (PDF), grow to advanced (.chaya)

  Implementation Details

  interface DocumentState {
    loaded: boolean;
    filename: string;
    pdfData: ArrayBuffer;
    annotations: Annotation[];
  }

  function renderSlots(state: DocumentState) {
    const chayaSlot = document.getElementById('chaya-slot');
    const pdfSlot = document.getElementById('pdf-slot');

    if (!state.loaded) {
      // Upload mode
      chayaSlot.innerHTML = `
        <div class="upload-slot" onclick="uploadChaya()">
          <div class="icon">📦</div>
          <div class="label">Upload .chaya</div>
        </div>
      `;
      pdfSlot.innerHTML = `
        <div class="upload-slot" onclick="uploadPdf()">
          <div class="icon">📄</div>
          <div class="label">Upload .pdf</div>
        </div>
      `;
    } else {
      // Download mode
      chayaSlot.innerHTML = `
        <div class="download-slot" onclick="downloadChaya()">
          <div class="icon">📦</div>
          <div class="label">Download .chaya</div>
        </div>
      `;
      pdfSlot.innerHTML = `
        <div class="download-slot" onclick="downloadPdf()">
          <div class="icon">📄</div>
          <div class="label">Download .pdf</div>
        </div>
      `;
    }
  }

  User Scenarios

  Scenario 1: New User
  - Sees two slots, uploads PDF
  - Works on annotations
  - Downloads .chaya file when done

  Scenario 2: Continuing Work
  - Uploads existing .chaya file
  - Continues where they left off
  - Downloads updated .chaya file

  Scenario 3: Sharing
  - Uploads .chaya file from colleague
  - Reviews/edits annotations
  - Downloads PDF for final distribution

  This approach eliminates the complexity of my earlier proposal while maintaining all the core functionality. Users would quickly learn this pattern and it scales from simple to advanced use cases naturally.

> Also we can remove the "Save annotations" button from the "Mark" view, as they will be packaged into the .chaya file.

⏺ Exactly! That's a great simplification. The "Save Annotations" button becomes redundant since saving is now unified into the .chaya download slot.

  Updated Mark Tab Interface

  Current Mark Tab:

  │ Annotations                                      │

  │ [annotation items...]                           │
  │                                                 │

  Simplified Mark Tab:
  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ Annotations                                      │
  │ 3 annotations                                   │
  │                                                 │
  │ [annotation items...]                           │
  │                                                 │
  │ (Auto-saved to .chaya download)                 │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Implementation Changes

  Remove from src/modes/annotator.ts:
  - saveAnnotationsBtn DOM element reference
  - Save button event listener
  - saveAnnotations() function
  - Save button UI updates

  The simplified flow becomes:
  1. User uploads PDF or .chaya → slots switch to download mode
  2. User marks annotations → changes auto-saved to memory
  3. User downloads .chaya → current state packaged and downloaded
  4. User downloads PDF → original PDF downloaded

  This also eliminates the "unsaved changes" workflow complexity since there's no explicit save step - everything flows through the download slots.

⏺ Implementation Steps

  Here's a logical progression to implement the .chaya file functionality:

  Phase 1: Dependencies and UI Foundation

  1. Add JSZip dependency - Need this for creating/reading ZIP files
  2. Design two-slot UI - Replace current file upload section with the cleaner upload/download slots
  3. Remove Save Annotations button - Clean up the Mark tab interface

  Phase 2: Core .chaya Functionality

  4. Implement .chaya file creation - Bundle PDF + annotations into ZIP format
  5. Implement .chaya file loading - Extract PDF + annotations from ZIP
  6. Update file loading system - Modify src/app.ts to handle both input types

  Phase 3: Integration and Testing

  7. Test complete workflow - Ensure round-trip works: PDF → annotate → .chaya → load → modify → .chaya

  Key Technical Decisions

  File Structure for .chaya:
  document.chaya (ZIP)
  ├── manifest.json     # Version, metadata
  ├── document.pdf      # Original PDF binary
  └── annotations.json  # Current annotation format

  UI State Management:
  - Track whether document is loaded
  - Switch slot modes (upload ↔ download) based on state
  - Auto-save annotations to memory (no explicit save)

  Backward Compatibility:
  - Keep existing PDF + JSON workflow working
  - .chaya format is additive enhancement
  - Same annotation data structure

       318 +      private async downloadPdfFile(): Promise<void> {
       319 +          if (!this.state.pdfFile) return;
       320 +          
       321 +          // Download the original PDF file
       322 +          const url = URL.createObjectURL(this.state.pdfFile);
       323 +          const a = document.createElement('a');
       324 +          a.href = url;
       325 +          a.download = this.state.pdfFile.name;
       326 +          document.body.appendChild(a);
       327 +          a.click();
       328 +          document.body.removeChild(a);
       329 +          URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

       324        private async downloadChayaFile(): Promise<void> {
       325 -          // TODO: Implement .chaya file creation and download
       326 -          console.log('Downloading .chaya file');
       327 -          // For now, just show an alert
       328 -          alert('Downloading .chaya files will be implemented in the next step');
       325 +          if (!this.state.pdfDocument || !this.state.pdfFile) {
       326 +              alert('No document loaded to create .chaya file');
       327 +              return;
       328 +          }
       329 +  
       330 +          try {
       331 +              console.log('Creating .chaya file...');
       332 +              
       333 +              // Create ZIP file
       334 +              const zip = new JSZip();
       335 +              
       336 +              // Add manifest.json
       337 +              const manifest = {
       338 +                  version: "1.0",
       339 +                  created: new Date().toISOString(),
       340 +                  originalFilename: this.state.pdfFile.name,
       341 +                  chayaFormatVersion: "1.0",
       342 +                  application: {
       343 +                      name: "Bookchop",
       344 +                      version: "1.0.0"
       345 +                  }
       346 +              };
       347 +              zip.file("manifest.json", JSON.stringify(manifest, null, 2));
       348 +              
       349 +              // Add original PDF
       350 +              const pdfArrayBuffer = await this.readFileAsArrayBuffer(this.state.pdfFile);
       351 +              zip.file("document.pdf", pdfArrayBuffer);
       352 +              
       353 +              // Add annotations.json
       354 +              const annotationsData = this.createAnnotationsJSON();
       355 +              zip.file("annotations.json", JSON.stringify(annotationsData, null, 2));
       356 +              
       357 +              // Generate ZIP file
       358 +              const chayaBlob = await zip.generateAsync({
       359 +                  type: "blob",
       360 +                  compression: "DEFLATE",
       361 +                  compressionOptions: {
       362 +                      level: 9
       363 +                  }
       364 +              });
       365 +              
       366 +              // Download the .chaya file
       367 +              const fileName = this.state.pdfFile.name.replace(/\.pdf$/i, '.chaya');
       368 +              const url = URL.createObjectURL(chayaBlob);
       369 +              const a = document.createElement('a');
       370 +              a.href = url;
       371 +              a.download = fileName;
       372 +              document.body.appendChild(a);
       373 +              a.click();
       374 +              document.body.removeChild(a);
       375 +              URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
       376 +              
       377 +              console.log('Successfully created .chaya file:', fileName);
       378 +              
       379 +          } catch (error) {
       380 +              console.error('Error creating .chaya file:', error);
       381 +              alert('Error creating .chaya file: ' + error);
       382 +          }
       383        }
