       316        // .chaya file handling methods (placeholder implementations)
       317        private async loadChayaFile(file: File): Promise<void> {
       318 -          // TODO: Implement .chaya file loading
       319 -          console.log('Loading .chaya file:', file.name);
       320 -          // For now, just show an alert
       321 -          alert('Loading .chaya files will be implemented in the next step');
       318 +          const loadingDiv = document.getElementById('app-loading') as HTMLDivElement;
       319 +          
       320 +          try {
       321 +              console.log('Loading .chaya file:', file.name);
       322 +              
       323 +              // Show loading progress
       324 +              loadingDiv.classList.remove('hidden');
       325 +              this.updateLoadingProgress(0, 'Loading .chaya file...', 'Reading ZIP file...');
       326 +              
       327 +              // Read ZIP file
       328 +              const zip = new JSZip();
       329 +              const zipContent = await zip.loadAsync(file);
       330 +              
       331 +              this.updateLoadingProgress(20, 'Extracting files...', 'Validating .chaya format...');
       332 +              
       333 +              // Validate required files
       334 +              const requiredFiles = ['manifest.json', 'document.pdf', 'annotations.json'];
       335 +              for (const requiredFile of requiredFiles) {
       336 +                  if (!zipContent.file(requiredFile)) {
       337 +                      throw new Error(`Invalid .chaya file: missing ${requiredFile}`);
       338 +                  }
       339 +              }
       340 +              
       341 +              this.updateLoadingProgress(40, 'Reading manifest...', 'Validating format version...');
       342 +              
       343 +              // Read and validate manifest
       344 +              const manifestText = await zipContent.file('manifest.json')!.async('string');
       345 +              const manifest = JSON.parse(manifestText);
       346 +              console.log('Manifest:', manifest);
       347 +              
       348 +              this.updateLoadingProgress(60, 'Extracting PDF...', 'Loading document content...');
       349 +              
       350 +              // Extract PDF data
       351 +              const pdfArrayBuffer = await zipContent.file('document.pdf')!.async('arraybuffer');
       352 +              const pdfBlob = new Blob([pdfArrayBuffer], { type: 'application/pdf' });
       353 +              const pdfFile = new File([pdfBlob], manifest.originalFilename || 'document.pdf', {
       354 +                  type: 'application/pdf'
       355 +              });
       356 +              
       357 +              this.updateLoadingProgress(80, 'Loading annotations...', 'Parsing annotation data...');
       358 +              
       359 +              // Extract annotations
       360 +              const annotationsText = await zipContent.file('annotations.json')!.async('string');
       361 +              const annotationsData = JSON.parse(annotationsText);
       362 +              const annotations = parseAnnotationsFromJson(annotationsData);
       363 +              
       364 +              this.updateLoadingProgress(90, 'Initializing document...', 'Setting up PDF viewer...');
       365 +              
       366 +              // Update state
       367 +              this.state.pdfFile = pdfFile;
       368 +              this.state.annotationsFile = null; // Not needed for .chaya files
       369 +              this.state.loadedAnnotations = annotations;
       370 +              this.state.loadedAnnotationsFileName = file.name;
       371 +              
       372 +              // Load PDF document
       373 +              const pdfjs = await waitForPdfjs();
       374 +              const loadingTask = pdfjs.getDocument(new Uint8Array(pdfArrayBuffer));
       375 +              this.state.pdfDocument = await loadingTask.promise;
       376 +              
       377 +              this.updateLoadingProgress(95, 'Finalizing...', 'Preparing user interface...');
       378 +              
       379 +              // Mark as loaded
       380 +              this.state.documentLoaded = true;
       381 +              this.state.hasUnsavedChanges = false;
       382 +              
       383 +              // Update slot UI to download mode
       384 +              this.updateSlotUI();
       385 +              
       386 +              this.updateLoadingProgress(100, 'Complete!', 'Chaya file loaded successfully');
       387 +              
       388 +              // Notify tabs that data is ready
       389 +              this.notifyTabsDataReady();
       390 +              
       391 +              // Listen for rendering completion
       392 +              this.waitForRenderingComplete();
       393 +              
       394 +              console.log('Successfully loaded .chaya file:', file.name);
       395 +              
       396 +          } catch (error) {
       397 +              console.error('Error loading .chaya file:', error);
       398 +              this.updateLoadingProgress(0, 'Error loading .chaya file', `Failed: ${error}`);
       399 +              setTimeout(() => {
       400 +                  loadingDiv.classList.add('hidden');
       401 +              }, 3000);
       402 +          }
       403        }

⏺ Perfect! The implementation is now complete. Let me summarize what we've accomplished:

  ✅ Phase 1 Complete: Two-Slot .chaya File Functionality

  What We've Implemented:

  1. ✅ JSZip Dependency - Added for handling .chaya ZIP files
  2. ✅ Two-Slot UI - Clean upload/download interface for .chaya and .pdf files
  3. ✅ Removed Save Button - Eliminated redundant "Save Annotations" button
  4. ✅ .chaya File Creation - Complete ZIP packaging with PDF + annotations + manifest
  5. ✅ .chaya File Loading - Full ZIP extraction and document restoration
  6. ✅ Unified File Loading - Single interface handles both PDF and .chaya inputs

  Key Features:

  Upload Mode (Empty State):
  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ Document                                         │
  ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │                                                 │
  │   ┌─────────────┐        ┌─────────────┐        │
  │   │     📦      │        │     📄      │        │
  │   │ Upload      │   OR   │ Upload      │        │
  │   │ .chaya      │        │ .pdf        │        │
  │   └─────────────┘        └─────────────┘        │
  │                                                 │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Download Mode (Document Loaded):
  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ Document: research_paper.pdf                    │
  ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │                                                 │
  │   ┌─────────────┐        ┌─────────────┐        │
  │   │     📦      │        │     📄      │        │
  │   │ Download    │        │ Download    │        │
  │   │ .chaya      │        │ .pdf        │        │
  │   └─────────────┘        └─────────────┘        │
  │                                                 │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  .chaya File Format:

  document.chaya (ZIP)
  ├── manifest.json     # Version, metadata, original filename
  ├── document.pdf      # Original PDF (raw binary, no encoding overhead)
  └── annotations.json  # Current annotation format

  User Workflow:

  1. Upload PDF → annotate → Download .chaya (complete package)
  2. Upload .chaya → continue editing → Download .chaya (updated package)
  3. Download .pdf at any time to get original document

  The implementation provides a clean, intuitive interface that maintains backward compatibility while adding the powerful single-file .chaya format. Users can now easily share complete annotated documents as single files, and the system
  handles the complexity of ZIP packaging/extraction transparently.

  ✅ Issue Fixed and Tested

  The problem was that the updateSlotUI() method was using innerHTML to replace the slot content, which was removing the hidden file input elements. I fixed this by:

  1. Preserving file input elements - Before updating innerHTML, I save references to the file input elements
  2. Re-attaching them - After updating the visual content, I append the file input elements back to their containers
  3. Maintaining event listeners - The change event listeners on the file inputs are preserved

  Test Results:

  - ✅ Chaya slot click triggers file input - Fixed and working

  - ✅ No JavaScript errors - Clean execution
  - ✅ Tab navigation works - Tab switching functional
  - ✅ Libraries load correctly - PDF.js and JSZip loading properly
  - ✅ UI switches to download mode - State transitions working

  The .chaya file functionality is now fully working with proper file input handling. Users can:

  1. Click PDF slot → triggers PDF file selection
  2. Click Chaya slot → triggers .chaya file selection
  3. Upload PDF → switches to download mode
  4. Upload .chaya → switches to download mode

  6. Download PDF → downloads original file
